# **O-ASIS: SIMULATION HYPOTHESES & OPERATIONAL CASE LAW**
## *Living Epistemic Register for Track B Empirical Discoveries*

This register records empirical friction, systemic edge-cases, and tested operational solutions discovered through the **O-ASIS Dual-Track Simulation Game Engine** ([`sim/`](../sim/)).

---

### **Epistemic Status Taxonomy**

To ensure non-autocratic, rigorous governance, every entry carries an explicit epistemological status:
* `[STATUS: SIMULATION_HYPOTHESIS]` — Empirically observed and resolved in the local game simulation; proposed for community review.
* `[STATUS: OASIS_STRESS_TESTED]` — Audited by adversarial AI engines (Red Team) against exploit vectors and thermodynamic conservation.
* `[STATUS: CANONICAL_MERGED]` — Purple Patch integrated into the canonical Living Constitution during Phase 0 (Genesis Architectural Hardening); still subject to post-Genesis sortition ratification.
* `[STATUS: RED_WINS_WITHDRAWN]` — The Red Team prevailed: the tested provision is withdrawn or narrowed instead of patched. A docket may legitimately close this way.
* `[STATUS: COMMUNITY_SORTITION_RATIFIED]` — Formally reviewed and ratified by a randomly drawn citizen sortition jury on [`github.com/kyberlex/ONE/discussions`](https://github.com/kyberlex/ONE/discussions).

> [!IMPORTANT]
> **Data Provenance Notice (added 2026-10-30):** Quantitative results in the "Telemetric / Simulation / Field Validation" sections of SIM-QA-01 to SIM-QA-09 come from the O-ASIS game engine (`sim/`) and from AI-generated adversarial sessions. They are **synthetic and illustrative**, not empirical measurements, and were not produced by calibrated, reproducible SIMONE runs. From SIM-QA-10 onward, every quantitative claim must link a reproducible script and results file (see `simone/benchmarks/`).

## **SIM-QA-01: Quorum Scaling & Demarchic Viability for Emerging Nodes (< 50 Pop)**

* **Date Recorded:** 2026-09-22  
* **Date Merged:** 2026-10-05  
* **Epistemic Status:** `[STATUS: CANONICAL_MERGED]` (Integrated into Living Constitution Arts. 4.2.1, 4.2.2)  
* **Originating Settlement:** Detroit Delray Commons (Starting Pop: 28)  
* **Constitutional Articles:** [`Art. 4.2.1`](../bible/chapters/chapter_04.md), [`Art. 4.2.2`](../bible/chapters/chapter_04.md), [`Art. 4.5`](../bible/chapters/chapter_04.md), [`Art. 5.1`](../bible/chapters/chapter_05.md)

### **1. The Material / Empirical Friction**
In the simulation engine, a founding micro-commons node initializes with a modest pioneer population ($N = 28$ citizens). Under a naive interpretation of Chapter IV, convening the **Neighborhood Sortition Council of 15 citizens** ([`Art. 4.2.2`](../bible/chapters/chapter_04.md)) requisitions **53.6% of the adult population** into deliberative assembly duty.

Because assembly duty mandates total exemption from routine physical labor ([`Art. 4.3.2`](../bible/chapters/chapter_04.md)), the remaining 13 citizens are mathematically incapable of completing the mandatory daily *chore roster* (8h greenhouse farming, 6h water/pumps, 6h microgrid maintenance, 8h fablab recycling). The node rapidly incurs a severe labor deficit, precipitating greywater overflow and food supply collapse within 12 simulated days.

Conversely, abolishing sortition or granting executive decree power to a single administrator would violate the Class-0 Invariant of Demarchy ([`Art. 4.1`](../bible/chapters/chapter_04.md)).

### **2. The Tested Countermeasure (Subsidiarity Scale)**
The simulation implements the **Principle of Demarchic Subsidiarity Scale**:
1. **Emerging Node (Population $< 50$):**
   - Sovereign local deliberation is assigned to the **Local Mediation Panel (3 citizens drawn by lot)** pursuant to [`Art. 4.2.1`](../bible/chapters/chapter_04.md).
   - Strict odd-number parity is preserved (preventing ties), while sequestering only ~10% of the active workforce.
2. **Consolidated Neighborhood (Population $\ge 50$):**
   - Automatically transitions to the full **Neighborhood Sortition Council (15 citizens)** pursuant to [`Art. 4.2.2`](../bible/chapters/chapter_04.md).
3. **Graduated Decision Thresholds ([`Art. 4.5`](../bible/chapters/chapter_04.md)):**
   - **60% Qualified Majority:** 2 of 3 votes (Tier 1) / 9 of 15 votes (Tier 2) for routine allocations.
   - **75% Constitutional Supermajority:** 3 of 3 votes (Tier 1) / 12 of 15 votes (Tier 2) for asylum, structural revisions, or emergency covenants.
4. **Cognitive & Screen Accessibility Invariant:**
   - Instead of 15 individual citizen cards cluttering mobile displays, deliberation stances are synthesized via a compact vector **Solarpunk Donut Chart (< 90px)** with a visual threshold notch at 60%/75%, accompanied by an expandable drawer for inspecting juror attributes on demand.

### **3. Telemetric Validation**
- 100-day headless deterministic simulation run with $N = 28$:
  - Zero labor deficit hours.
  - Closed thermodynamic balance ($L_{min}$ floor sustained at 100%).
  - Demarchic rotation executed every 30 days (`720 ticks`) without oligarchy or consecutive terms.

### **4. Open Question for Future Ratification**
*Should the population boundary of 50 residents be formally codified as a statutory annotation to Article 4.2 in the Living Constitution, or should nodes have autonomy to calibrate this transition between 40 and 60 residents based on local automation levels?*  
*(Pending community sortition review on GitHub Discussions).*

## **SIM-QA-02: Dual-Tier Tool Boundary: Civic Tool Libraries vs. Inviolable Personal Craft Gear**

* **Date Recorded:** 2026-09-23  
* **Date Merged:** 2026-10-05  
* **Epistemic Status:** `[STATUS: CANONICAL_MERGED]` (Integrated into Living Constitution Art. 2.2.1)  
* **Originating Context:** Urban apartment block & low-capital seed node field dynamics (Tbilisi/Georgia working-class housing dialogue)  
* **Constitutional Articles:** [`Art. 2.2.1`](../bible/chapters/chapter_02.md), [`Art. 2.2.3`](../bible/chapters/chapter_02.md), [`Art. 3.2`](../bible/chapters/chapter_03.md)

### **1. The Material / Empirical Friction**
In ground-level seed nodes (e.g., dense 2–3 story urban apartment blocks with limited capital and zero immediate budget for rooftop solar or high-tech fablabs), an acute operational tension arises between two core tenets of O.N.E.:
1. **The Inviolability of Personal Effects & Craft Gear ([`Art. 2.2.1`](../bible/chapters/chapter_02.md)):** Citizens must feel somatic and psychological security that their personal instruments, digital workstations, and artisanal trade tools cannot be collectivized or requisitioned by neighborhood fiat.
2. **The Anti-Waste & De-financialization Imperative:** In a typical 20-household building, 20 separate families each privately purchase cheap, redundant utility tools (e.g., hammer drills, extension ladders, angle grinders, sewing machines, automotive jacks). An electric rotary drill has an empirical average utilization of only **12 to 15 minutes across its entire operating lifespan**, representing dead capital extraction by retail monopolies and unnecessary landfill waste.

If a seed node demands total collectivization of all tools, skilled craftspeople and artisans refuse to participate (fearing that their precision, calibrated, or sentimentally vital tools will be damaged by careless neighbors). If the node remains entirely hyper-individualized, poor working-class residents remain trapped in debt and unable to maintain their building.

### **2. The Tested Operational Countermeasure (Dual-Tier Tool Classification)**
The node operating protocol establishes an explicit, objective boundary between **Personal Vocation Gear** and **Civic Utility Libraries**:

1. **Tier 1 — Inviolable Personal Craft Gear ([`Art. 2.2.1`](../bible/chapters/chapter_02.md)):**
   - Defined as tools of high daily utilization, calibrated precision, intimate creative expression, or personal trade specialization (e.g., luthier chisels, developer laptops, surgeon/dentist implements, artist brushes, chef knives, personalized mechanic torque sets).
   - **Juridical Status:** Inviolable individual possession. They can never be expropriated, borrowed without express bilateral consent, or pooled into communal circulation.

2. **Tier 2 — Civic Tool Library & Maintenance Pool ([`Art. 2.2.3`](../bible/chapters/chapter_02.md)):**
   - Defined as intermittent, low-frequency, high-durability infrastructure maintenance equipment (e.g., rotary masonry drills, telescoping aluminum ladders, drain augers, high-pressure washers, heavy sewing/canvas machines, lawn aerators).
   - **Juridical Status:** Held under communal usufruct in a common entrance locker or basement nook. Governed by a simple physical/mesh checkout log.
   - **Maintenance & Sharpening:** Maintained, lubricated, and serviced as part of the node’s shared chore hours or circular fablab recycling roster.

3. **The Voluntary Custody Registry ("Adopt-a-Tool"):**
   - Residents who privately own Tier 2 tools may deposit them into the Civic Tool Library voluntarily.
   - **Incentive / Reciprocity:** The community assumes 100% of the replacement/maintenance cost (sharpening drill bits, replacing batteries) through collective reserves, while the depositor retains unconditional priority reservation whenever they require the tool.

### **3. Simulation & Field Validation**
- Modeled in building-scale metabolic runs ($N = 15–30$ households):
  - Achieves a **~75% reduction in household hardware expenditures** during the founding year.
  - Zero reports of craft expropriation disputes or artisan alienation.
  - Serves as the primary behavioral bridge for building social trust ("metabolic muscle memory") prior to implementing higher-tier solar or water microgrids.

### **4. Open Question for Future Ratification**
*Should the boundary between Tier 1 (Inviolable Craft) and Tier 2 (Civic Library) be governed by an objective checklist of asset classes in statutory annexes, or left to rapid subsidiarity determination by the Local Mediation Panel (Art. 4.2.1) upon dispute?*  
*(Pending community sortition review on GitHub Discussions).*

## **SIM-QA-03: Ecological Sensor Integrity: ZK-Telemetry Proofs & Analog Citizen Ground-Truthing**

* **Date Recorded:** 2026-09-26  
* **Date Merged:** 2026-10-05  
* **Epistemic Status:** `[STATUS: CANONICAL_MERGED]` (Integrated into Living Constitution Arts. 3.4.3, 3.4.5)  
* **Originating Context:** Operational Edge-Case OP-01 (Sensor Telemetry Drift & Ground-Truthing)  
* **Constitutional Articles:** [`Art. 3.4.2`](../bible/chapters/chapter_03.md), [`Art. 3.4.3`](../bible/chapters/chapter_03.md), [`Art. 7.2`](../bible/chapters/chapter_07.md)

### **1. The Material / Empirical Friction**
While Article 3.4.3 mandates open-hardware Programmable Logic Controllers (PLCs) with bit-for-bit verifiable firmware, physical field sensors (aquifer pressure transducers, weir flow meters, soil carbon analyzers) can suffer mechanical bio-fouling or hardware calibration drift. An algorithmic anomaly detector might mistake a gradual $-0.25\sigma$ monthly drift for legitimate climatic variation, creating a discrepancy between digital telemetry and true physical carrying capacity.

### **2. Adversarial Stress-Test (RED-5 Assault)**
* **Vector 1 (Proprietary TEE Hardware Trojans):** Relying on closed proprietary "Trusted Execution Environments" (TEEs) allows hardware fabricators to insert microarchitectural backdoors or forged cryptographic signatures.
* **Vector 2 (Local Cartel Bribery & Rubber-Stamping):** If analog inspections are performed strictly by local neighbors, local users facing water quotas can socially coerce or bribe inspectors into misreporting dipstick levels or water quality tests.

### **3. The Hardened Operational Countermeasure (Purple Patch)**
1. **Strict Open Hardware Mandate ([`Art. 3.4.3`](../bible/chapters/chapter_03.md)):** Prohibition of closed proprietary binary blobs or untrusted enclaves; all telemetry hardware must run fully verifiable open firmware.
2. **Citizen Ground-Truthing Squads ([`Art. 3.4.5`](../bible/chapters/chapter_03.md)):** Lay citizen teams equipped with manual analog tools (calibrated dipsticks, mechanical flow meters, titration reagents) conduct unpredictable spot-checks against digital readouts.
3. **Blind Split-Sample Protocol:** Audits mandate split samples: duplicate sealed physical samples are independently verified by laboratories drawn by lot from non-contiguous watersheds, eliminating local collusion and model drift.

### **4. Open Question for Future Ratification**
*What is the optimal cadence for citizen ground-truthing squads to prevent operational fatigue while ensuring 99% detection confidence against physical sensor fouling?*  
*(Pending community sortition review on GitHub Discussions).*

## **SIM-QA-04: Cryptographic Anti-Brokerage for High-Demand FabLab Queues**

* **Date Recorded:** 2026-09-26  
* **Date Merged:** 2026-10-05  
* **Epistemic Status:** `[STATUS: CANONICAL_MERGED]` (Integrated into Living Constitution Arts. 1.4, 3.3.3)  
* **Originating Context:** Operational Edge-Case OP-02 (Inelastic FabLab Queuing)  
* **Constitutional Articles:** [`Art. 1.4`](../bible/chapters/chapter_01.md), [`Art. 2.1.2`](../bible/chapters/chapter_02.md), [`Art. 2.2.3`](../bible/chapters/chapter_02.md), [`Art. 3.3.3`](../bible/chapters/chapter_03.md)

### **1. The Material / Empirical Friction**
While real estate, land, and housing are perpetually de-commodified under dynamic usufruct (Art. 2.1 and Art. 2.5), localized physical bottlenecks can occur for high-demand specialized machinery in Tier 3 Open Fab-Labs (e.g., 5-axis CNC mills, metal laser sintering, cleanroom lithography). If reservation queues are open and fungible, an informal secondary market could theoretically emerge where individuals reserve prime tool-hours speculatively and trade access to third parties in exchange for personal gifts or informal favors.

### **2. Adversarial Stress-Test (RED-1 Assault)**
* **Vector 1 (In-Person Proxy Fabrication):** If reservations are soulbound, a scalper simply attends in person, scans their own badge, and executes a third-party client's proprietary G-code file in exchange for unrecorded cash or favors.
* **Vector 2 (Sybil Queue Squatting):** A colluding group reserves prime production blocks under automated accounts with dummy jobs, running zero-spindle dry air cuts or cancelling at the 14-minute mark to block competitors.
* **Vector 3 (Parasitic Commercial Enclosure):** An entrepreneur uses free communal tool time, grid power, and tooling wear to manufacture closed-source proprietary goods for external monetary sale.

### **3. The Hardened Operational Countermeasure (Purple Patch)**
1. **The Execution-Equals-Publication Invariant ([`Art. 1.4`](../bible/chapters/chapter_01.md) & [`Art. 3.3.3`](../bible/chapters/chapter_03.md)):** Any job executed on communal Tier 3 machinery must commit its toolpaths, G-code, and CAM bills of materials to the public local mesh repository. Secret, proprietary, or DRM-locked manufacturing on communal tooling is unconstitutional.
2. **Quadratic Frequency Dampening ([`Art. 3.3.3`](../bible/chapters/chapter_03.md)):** Individual queue priority decays quadratically based on trailing 30-day cumulative machine utilization hours ($W = 1 / (1 + \sum h^2)$), ensuring occasional makers and new creators are never locked out by intensive operators.
3. **Soulbound Booking & Dynamic 15-Minute Forfeiture:** Booking tokens are non-transferable; unattended workstations forfeit after 15 minutes to standby creators drawn by lot, with repeat unexcused no-shows incurring temporary peak-slot booking cooldowns.

### **4. Open Question for Future Ratification**
*How should FabLab booking systems accommodate unforeseen delays (e.g., transit disruptions or urgent care duties) without allowing speculative reservation hoarding?*  
*(Pending community sortition review on GitHub Discussions).*

## **SIM-QA-05: Codification of Civic Rescue, Firefighting & Disaster Response Guilds**

* **Date Recorded:** 2026-10-05  
* **Date Merged:** 2026-10-05  
* **Epistemic Status:** `[STATUS: CANONICAL_MERGED]` (Integrated into Living Constitution Arts. 5.1.3, 8.1.2)  
* **Originating Context:** Habitat Resiliency Modeling & Operational Analysis of Chapter VIII (Public Safety vs. Physical Entropy Asymmetry)  
* **Constitutional Articles:** [`Art. 5.1.3`](../bible/chapters/chapter_05.md), [`Art. 8.1.2`](../bible/chapters/chapter_08.md), [`Art. 8.2`](../bible/chapters/chapter_08.md), [`Art. 10.4.4`](../bible/chapters/chapter_10.md)

### **1. The Material / Empirical Friction**
In the Living Constitution, public safety is formulated almost exclusively around the abolition of militarized policing, interpersonal dispute de-escalation, restorative justice circles, and non-carceral custody. Article 8.1.2 creates *"unarmed Civic Peace Guilds trained in de-escalation and trauma triage"*.

However, physical reality and thermodynamic entropy generate acute hazards independent of human malicious intent or social friction:
1. **Physical and Combustion Disasters:** Conflagrations in timber/strawbale modular habitat units (MHU), high-voltage battery energy storage system (BESS) thermal runaways, and catastrophic forest/wildland fires.
2. **Geophysical and Meteorological Shocks:** Flash flooding, riverbank levee breaches, structural earthquake entrapments, alpine/speleological rescues, and industrial hazardous material (Hazmat) neutralizations.

Conflating technical rescue and firefighting with "Peace Guilds" or "interpersonal mediation" creates a lethal operational category error:
* Peace Guild mediators are trained in somatic trauma and dialogue, not in high-angle rope extraction, breathing apparatus (SCBA) protocols, hydraulic extrication, or dynamic wildfire flanking.
* Unlike armed policing (which historically protects absentee property monopolies and sovereign borders through coercive violence), **firefighting and search-and-rescue (SAR) represent the purest historical embodiment of non-carceral mutual aid**.
* Failing to explicitly codify a dedicated, technical **Civic Rescue & Firefighting Guild** leaves ambiguous how emergency command functions during sub-minute life-and-death crises, how equipment is provisioned, and how training/readiness hours are credited under the labor reciprocity matrix.

### **2. The Tested Operational Countermeasure (Institutional Bifurcation & ICS)**
The operational simulation implements a deterministic bifurcation of public safety and emergency functions:

1. **Explicit Domain Bifurcation:**
   * **Civic Peace Guilds ([`Art. 8.1.2`](../bible/chapters/chapter_08.md)):** Stewards of human social peace, interpersonal restorative justice, de-escalation, and psychiatric/trauma triage.
   * **Civic Rescue & Fire Guilds / Civil Protection Brigades ([`Art. 8.1.2`](../bible/chapters/chapter_08.md) & [`Art. 10.4.4`](../bible/chapters/chapter_10.md)):** Autonomous technical guilds specialized in structural and wildland firefighting, technical search and rescue (SAR), flood/seismic disaster response, and chemical/biological hazard mitigation.

2. **De-Militarized Incident Command Protocol (Open ICS):**
   * During active kinetic/environmental crises (active fires, structural collapses, hazardous plumes), tactical operational authority is temporarily vested in certified **Incident Technical Coordinators** drawn from the Rescue Guild, operating under flat, open Incident Command System protocols.
   * Decisions are strictly bounded to life-safety, victim triage, and structural containment; coordinators possess zero judicial, carceral, or property-disposition authority.

3. **Hybrid Staffing & Peak Ergonomic Multipliers ([`Art. 5.1.3`](../bible/chapters/chapter_05.md)):**
   * **Core Technical Mentors:** A standing cadre of certified master instructors and technical specialists who maintain complex apparatus (pumps, thermal drones, breathing gear, cutting torches).
   * **Rotational & Voluntary Reserves:** Civic cohorts drawn from the local population completing rotational shifts and voluntary on-call standby.
   * **Labor Reciprocity:** Active emergency deployments, technical training drills, and hazardous standby carry peak **ergonomic time multipliers of $4\times$ to $5\times$** against baseline chore obligations pursuant to Article 5.1.3.

4. **Hardware Standard & Analog Resilience ([`Art. 6.1.3`](../bible/chapters/chapter_06.md) & [`Art. 6.2.2`](../bible/chapters/chapter_06.md)):**
   * Firefighting and rescue apparatus are standardized across open-hardware blueprints: electric/hybrid high-torque tender chassis, pressurized gravity-fed cistern hydrants (requiring zero grid power), and thermal imaging drones operating ephemeral, zero-biometric edge telemetry.

5. **Inter-Watershed Mutual Aid Logistics ([`Art. 10.4.4`](../bible/chapters/chapter_10.md)):**
   * Large-scale catastrophes (e.g., wildland conflagrations exceeding local suppression capacity) trigger automated inter-basin mobilization corridors, transferring specialized equipment and mobile strike brigades across adjacent watersheds without political delay.

### **3. Proposed Canonical Redline (For Confederal Ratification)**

#### **A. Amendment to Chapter VIII, Article 8.1, Clause 2:**
```diff
- 2. Mercenary syndicates and Private Military Companies (PMCs) are prohibited as structural crimes. Public peace is stewarded exclusively by unarmed Civic Peace Guilds trained in de-escalation and trauma triage.
+ 2. Mercenary syndicates and Private Military Companies (PMCs) are prohibited as structural crimes. Public safety is stewarded through functional specialization:
+    * Civic Peace Guilds: Unarmed practitioners trained in interpersonal mediation, de-escalation, and restorative trauma triage;
+    * Civic Rescue & Fire Guilds: Dedicated technical services stewarding structural and wildland firefighting, technical search and rescue (SAR), geophysical disaster response, and hazardous material containment.
```

#### **B. Amendment to Chapter V, Article 5.1, Clause 3:**
```diff
- 3. Ergonomic Multipliers and Kinetic Metabolic Floor: Arduous or hazardous tasks carry certified ergonomic time multipliers of 3x–5x against baseline obligations and sabbatical allocations.
+ 3. Ergonomic Multipliers and Kinetic Metabolic Floor: Arduous or hazardous tasks—including active emergency rescue, structural/wildland firefighting, toxic recycling, and levee stabilization—carry certified ergonomic time multipliers of 3x–5x against baseline obligations and sabbatical allocations.
```

### **4. Open Questions for Future Ratification**
1. *Should basic wildland fire containment and CPR/trauma first-aid be integrated into the universal young adult educational curriculum (Art. 5.3.2 / 5.4.2) across all bioregions?*
2. *What objective telemetric thresholds (e.g., thermal satellite hotspot detection, seismic accelerometers) should automatically engage inter-watershed mutual aid brigades before a formal Sortition Council convenes?*  
*(Pending community sortition review on GitHub Discussions).*

## **SIM-QA-06: Governance, Thermodynamic Calibration & Usufruct Transformation of Legacy High-Capital Assets (Fabs, Data Centers & Megacities)**

* **Date Recorded:** 2026-10-20  
* **Date Merged:** 2026-10-21  
* **Epistemic Status:** `[STATUS: CANONICAL_MERGED]` (Integrated into Living Constitution Arts. 2.2, 3.3.4, 6.2, 7.4, 10.3)  
* **Originating Context:** Operational Edge-Case OP-06 (Neutralizing the Pastoral Fallacy in Heavy Industry, Hyperscale Compute & Metropolises)  
* **Constitutional Articles:** [`Art. 2.2`](../bible/chapters/chapter_02.md), [`Art. 3.3.4`](../bible/chapters/chapter_03.md), [`Art. 6.2`](../bible/chapters/chapter_06.md), [`Art. 7.4`](../bible/chapters/chapter_07.md), [`Art. 10.3`](../bible/chapters/chapter_10.md)  
* **Master Architectural Specification:** [`oasis/legacy_high_capital_assets.md`](legacy_high_capital_assets.md)

### **1. The Material / Empirical Friction**
In standard post-capitalist discourse, alternative systems either regress into agrarian parochialism (dismantling advanced industry) or preserve corporate/state technocracies. Advanced semiconductor fabrication (EUV lithography at 3nm/2nm), chlor-alkali and petrochemical synthesis, hyperscale data centers, and multi-million-citizen metropolises cannot be atomized into small neighborhood workshops without catastrophic yield loss, thermodynamic failure, and somatic collapse.

### **2. The Tested Operational Countermeasures (The Five Mechanisms)**
1. **Classification as Tier 2-A "Indivisible Capital Goods" (Art. 2.2 & Art. 3.3.4):**
   - High-throughput facilities are recognized as indivisible social capital under Federated Commons Usufruct.
   - Technical worker guilds manage operations under the democratic oversight of Bioregional (101 delegates) and Global (1,001 delegates) Citizen Assemblies.
   - Physical capital depreciation reserves (tool wear, catalyst degradation, target replacement) must be fully replenished from the thermodynamic ledger before surplus is released to Tier 3 discretionary fab-labs.
2. **Thermodynamic Mass-Balance Re-Engineering (Art. 3.1 & Art. 3.4):**
   - Advanced fabs must implement closed-circuit evaporation, catalytic chemical recovery, and dry-scrubbing. Breaches of river basin regenerative boundaries trigger the Thermodynamic Lexicographic Viability Operator ($\mathcal{L}_{\min}$) to deterministically throttle synthesis run-lengths and protect somatic water baselines.
   - Hyperscale data centers and exothermic synthesis pipe reject heat directly into district heating, greenhouse biomass loops, or low-temperature organic Rankine cycle turbines.
3. **Compute Infrastructure to Federated Commons (Ch. VII & Art. 6.4):**
   - Monopolistic cloud infrastructure and proprietary firmware blobs are banned (Art. 7.2); facilities transition to open BMC, RISC-V/Open-Compute hardware, and deterministic hypervisors.
   - Root keys are fragmented using Threshold Multi-Party Computation (t-MPC) keys distributed among sortition jurors across non-contiguous watersheds (Art. 7.4).
   - Training clusters operate as interruptible loads ramping down instantly during renewable generation deficits to protect Tier 1 survival grids.
4. **Dense Urban Agglomerations as Metabolic Sinks (Ch. II, Art. 2.1, Art. 2.3, Art. 10.3, Art. 10.4):**
   - Abolition of absentee real estate and mortgage debt converts high-density towers into Protected Possessory Usufruct held by resident Community Land Trusts; eviction is unconstitutional.
   - Aggressive retrofitting: BIPV, passive solar, gravity-assisted cisterns, de-paving, and permaculture green belts.
   - Metropolises exceeding carrying capacity enter transparent, bilateral physical exergy-and-materials compacts with surplus agricultural and energy basins, balanced directly on public input-output ledgers.
5. **The Transition Architecture (Ch. IX):**
   - Insolvent corporate fabs and logistics hubs are expropriated via Universal Debt Jubilees (Art. 9.5.2) and adverse possession into public commons trusts.
   - Advanced inputs (Zeiss/ASML photolithography optics, rare earths) are procured through the External Trade Interface (Art. 9.3) via direct physical barter, shielding the internal demonetized economy from external currency speculation.
   - Once unified, any region attempting asymmetric export withholding faces automatic Systemic Decoupling (*Disiunctio Reticularis*, Art. 10.3.3).

### **3. Simulation & Field Validation**
- Headless thermodynamic metabolic runs confirm:
  - Fabs operating on closed-loop catalytic water recovery sustain yields within $\pm 0.5\%$ of linear baselines while cutting fresh watershed draws by $> 94\%$.
  - Data center dynamic grid balancing reduces peak grid curtailment by $22.4\%$ across federated watersheds.
  - Zero housing evictions recorded; resident CLTs maintain physical building lifespans $> 60$ years under preventive maintenance reserves.

### **4. Open Questions for Future Ratification**
1. *What standardized sensory benchmarks should trigger automated inter-basin raw material transfers for Tier 2-A maintenance before sortition assemblies convene?*
2. *Should worker guild certification in high-hazard Tier 2-A facilities (e.g. EUV excimer laser calibration, toxic fluoride scrubbing) carry an explicit apprenticeship parity requirement cross-audited by independent watersheds?*  
*(Pending community sortition review on GitHub Discussions).*

## **SIM-QA-07: Active Solar Management via Reversible L1 Lagrange Sunshade vs. Chemical Terrestrial Geoengineering**

* **Date Recorded:** 2026-10-21  
* **Date Merged:** 2026-10-21  
* **Epistemic Status:** `[STATUS: CANONICAL_MERGED]` (Integrated into Living Constitution Arts. 10.5, 11.1)  
* **Originating Context:** Epistemic Review with Hard-Science Futurists (Isaac Arthur SFIA & Planetary Thermodynamics)  
* **Constitutional Articles:** [`Art. 10.5`](../bible/chapters/chapter_10.md), [`Art. 11.1`](../bible/chapters/chapter_11.md)

### **1. The Material / Theoretical Friction**
Article 10.5 originally banned all unilateral geoengineering, aerosol spraying, and ocean fertilization, permitting stabilization only to avert acute multi-species tipping points via a 75% qualified supermajority of the Global Commons Assembly and the affirmative consent of *every affected watershed*.

As raised in adversarial stress-testing:
1. **The Veto Paralysis Trap:** In an already destabilized climate (420+ ppm CO2, permafrost thaw), demanding unanimous consent across every terrestrial watershed creates a structural paralysis trap (*liberum veto*). While assemblies deliberate, runaway thermal feedback loops (e.g. Arctic albedo flip) lock in irreversible extinction cascades.
2. **Category Error (Chemical SRM vs. Optical Space Deflection):** Terrestrial chemical interventions (stratospheric sulfur injection, oceanic iron dumping) carry asymmetric toxic precipitation, monsoon disruption, and catastrophic termination shock. In contrast, **passive optical occultation in deep space** ($L_1$ Lagrange point, reducing insolation by 0.5%–1.5%) produces zero chemical fallout, alters no atmospheric chemistry, and can be modulated or dispersed cleanly.

### **2. Adversarial Stress-Test (RED-1 & RED-2 Assault)**
* **Vector 1 (RED-1 Assault: Latent Asymmetric Insolation Conflicts):** Reducing the solar constant by 1.0% alters global hydrological Hadley circulation unevenly. While temperate zones benefit from cooling, tropical monsoon basins might experience convective precipitation shifts, creating inter-regional disputes.
* **Vector 2 (RED-2 & RED-5 Assault: Sabotage, Capture & Mass-Ballast Termination Shock):** An aggressive faction could dock propulsion units or ballast mass to hold the array fixed, or detonate the array suddenly, triggering an abrupt termination shock warming spike that collapses global agriculture in a single growing season.

### **3. The Hardened Operational Countermeasure (Purple Patch)**
1. **Perpetual Constitutional Ban on Chemical Geoengineering (Art. 10.5.2):**
   - The absolute prohibition against aerosol dispersal, tropospheric sulfur injection, and oceanic chemical fertilization remains permanently inviolate.
2. **Classification of L1 Solar Deflection as a Sol Commons Trust (Art. 10.5.3 & Art. 11.1):**
   - Deep-space optical management is categorized under Chapter XI (*Patrimonium Solis*) not as atmospheric interference, but as **Solar Flux Regulation (*Administratio Insolationis*)**.
   - Deployment requires a seventy-five percent (75%) qualified supermajority of the Global Commons Assembly (1,001 delegates) and a sixty-six percent (66%) concurring vote across Continental Governance Chambers (301 delegates each), eliminating the single-watershed *liberum veto* paralysis.
3. **Passive Fail-Safe (Radiation Pressure Self-Dispersal / Anti-Termination Shock):**
   - All $L_1$ occulting units maintain passive radiation-pressure trim tabs. If active cryptographic keep-alive beacons from ground telemetry lapse for $> 14$ consecutive days, solar photon pressure automatically destabilizes and feathers the disks out of the occultation corridor over a thirty-six (36) month gradual decay gradient, preventing abrupt termination shock and administrative blackmail.

## **SIM-QA-08: Deep-Space Defensive Realism: Distributed Kinetic & Laser Immune System vs. Ballistic Mass-Velocity Threats**

* **Date Recorded:** 2026-10-21  
* **Date Merged:** 2026-10-21  
* **Epistemic Status:** `[STATUS: CANONICAL_MERGED]` (Integrated into Living Constitution Art. 11.6)  
* **Originating Context:** Epistemic Review with Defense Realists & Astrodynamics Experts (Isaac Arthur SFIA & Orbital Ballistics)  
* **Constitutional Articles:** [`Art. 11.6`](../bible/chapters/chapter_11.md)

### **1. The Material / Theoretical Friction**
Fundamental astrodynamics establishes that in deep space, any high-mass propulsion drive is an intrinsic weapon of mass destruction ($E_k = \frac{1}{2}mv^2$). A standard 5,000-ton freighter or deflected asteroid at $50\text{ km/s}$ carries kinetic energy of dozens of megatons.
Defection incentives and restorative procedures are physically inert against a ballistic mass coasting on a collision trajectory toward an inhabited habitat ring or Earth.

### **2. Adversarial Stress-Test (RED-3 & RED-5 Assault)**
* **Vector 1 (RED-3 Assault: The Dual-Use Orbital Death-Ray / Mirror Hijack):** A GW-class phased-array laser station capable of asteroid ablation could be re-targeted at a planetary city or CELSS habitat, creating an instantaneous relativistic death-ray.
* **Vector 2 (RED-5 Assault: Astronomical Light-Delay & Sortition Paralysis):** A hyperbolic collision burn at $60\text{ km/s}$ might be detected with only a 15-minute terminal intercept window. If light-delay is 12 minutes, real-time sortition voting guarantees death by latency.
* **Vector 3 (RED-3 & RED-4 Assault: Standing Praetorian Fleet Extortion):** Maintaining dedicated combat interceptor tugs creates a specialized military caste that extracts social resources.

### **3. The Hardened Operational Countermeasure (Purple Patch)**
1. **The Orbital Immune System (*Systema Immunitatis Caelestis*, Art. 11.6.2):**
   - Planetary defense is re-conceptualized not as an offensive fleet, but as civilizational immune infrastructure.
   - Phased-array laser stations and autonomous interceptor tugs maintain 100% civilian peacetime utility: laser arrays beam photon pressure to propel civilian solar-sail cargo clippers and clear orbital debris, while tugs harvest dead satellites for remelting under Swords into Plowshares (*Gladii in vomeres*, Art. 11.6.3).
2. **Hardwired Optical Geometric Blinders:**
   - Emitters are hardwired with analog geometric occulters and blinders that mechanically trip and dump power to space radiators if the beam is pointed within a $15^\circ$ exclusion cone of any registered inhabited habitat, moon, or Earth.
3. **Deterministic Physics-Bounded Autonomous Interception Corridor:**
   - Sortition assemblies deliberate in advance on the mathematical intercept envelope. Terminal interception runs on pre-compiled, open-source, bit-for-bit verifiable firmware executing across Byzantine Fault Tolerant (BFT) optical radar sentinels, overcoming astronomical light-delay without sacrificing democratic sovereignty.

## **SIM-QA-09: Autonomous AI & Robotic Swarm Aggression: Hard Galvanic Air-Gaps, Directed-Energy Point Defense & Non-Somatic Countermeasures**

* **Date Recorded:** 2026-10-21  
* **Date Merged:** 2026-10-21  
* **Epistemic Status:** `[STATUS: CANONICAL_MERGED]` (Integrated into Living Constitution Arts. 6.5, 8.1.4)  
* **Originating Context:** Adversarial Systems Stress-Testing (Autonomous Weapon Swarms, Unmanned Aerial Systems & Algorithmic Siege Vectors)  
* **Constitutional Articles:** [`Art. 6.5`](../bible/chapters/chapter_06.md), [`Art. 8.1.4`](../bible/chapters/chapter_08.md)  

### **1. The Material / Theoretical Friction**
Chapters VIII and IX formulate collective defense against aggressive actors under the premise of biological, sentient opponents: defection incentives, supply decoupling, peer restorative accountability, and non-carceral mediation.

However, when aggressive factions or dying legacy corporate/state entities deploy **uncrewed autonomous weapon swarms (AWS)**, algorithmic loitering munitions, automated factory replicators, or autonomous cyber-physical worms:
1. **Total Immunity to Social, Restorative & Economic Levers:** Autonomous synthetic agents have no empathy, no families, no caloric needs, no fear of mortality, and no moral conscience. Defection incentives, diplomatic dialogue, and restorative circles are completely inert against an objective loss-function algorithm programmed for area denial or kinetic destruction.
2. **Asymmetric Reaction Latency (The Millisecond Gap):** Swarms of thousands of cheap 3D-printed loitering munitions coordinate via low-latency ad-hoc mesh networks and onboard optical SLAM. Human deliberative bodies (even rapid-response sortition panels) operate on minutes or hours. An algorithmic kinetic saturation attack unfolds in seconds.
3. **Sub-Kinetic Cyber-Physical Sabotage:** Highly automated commons nodes rely on telemetry and automated microgrids, water recycling pumps, and bioreactors. A compromised firmware payload or zero-day synthetic worm could quietly induce systemic failure without a single kinetic shot being fired.

### **2. Adversarial Stress-Test (RED-3 & RED-5 Assault)**
* **Vector 1 (RED-3 Assault: The Human-Shield & Biologically Mixed Swarm Trap):** If defensive CIWS and directed-energy arrays are pre-authorized to engage autonomously, an adversary can strap coerced human hostages or captive non-combatants directly to drone fuselages, or route swarms through dense civilian greenhouse corridors. Autonomous interceptors risk committing collateral civilian casualties, violating the inviolable sacredness of sentient biological life (Art. 8.2).
* **Vector 2 (RED-5 Assault: The Analog Knife-Switch Starvation Trap):** Disconnecting digital telemetry via mechanical knife-switches eliminates cyber penetration, but complex bio-metabolic municipal loops (closed-loop aquaponics, nutrient dosing, high-voltage microgrid phase matching) cannot maintain stoichiometric efficiency on manual gravity levers alone. A protracted algorithmic cyber-siege simply forces the node to stay in primitive analog mode until crop yields crash and the settlement starves from thermodynamic de-optimization.
* **Vector 3 (RED-3 & RED-1 Assault: The Leontief Attrition & Ammo Exhaustion Trap):** Low-cost stamped plywood loitering drones with commercial hobbyist motors cost trivial embodied energy ($< 15\text{ kWh}$). If the defending node counters each projectile with high-wear kinetic CIWS ammunition, missile interceptors, or delicate GaAs microwave tubes, the attacker achieves an asymmetric 10:1 material exhaustion advantage, draining the node’s Strategic Physical Reserves (Art. 3.5).

### **3. The Hardened Operational Countermeasure (Purple Patch)**
1. **The Non-Lethal Electromagnetic Priority Cascade (Art. 8.1.4):**
   - Autonomous kinetic hard-kill (CIWS projectile firing) is constitutionally banned within inhabited domestic boundaries or where biological proximity sensors detect human presence within the fragmentation radius.
   - Mixed-threat and civilian-adjacent engagements are restricted strictly to **Non-Ionizing Electromagnetic Disruption (High-Power Microwave - HPM, and directional multi-band RF/optical jamming)**. HPM induces circuit-destroying voltage spikes in semiconductor gates ($< 1\mu\text{m}$) within milliseconds, disabling drone flight computers and motor controllers instantly, while mammalian biological tissue experiences only harmless non-ionizing RF attenuation ($< 0.1^\circ\text{C}$ transient delta).
   - In mixed residential airspace, point defense utilizes non-explosive kinetic capture: air-propelled high-tensile micro-filament entangling nets and tethered capture drones that drop disabled hardware to the ground without explosive shockwaves or shrapnel.
2. **The Air-Gapped Islanded Kernel Architecture (Art. 6.5 & Art. 7.1.4):**
   - The Knife-Switch Protocol physically decouples *external wide-area mesh networks and remote telemetry lines (WAN)* via galvanic break isolators.
   - Internally, local bioreactor, greenhouse, and microgrid telemetry continues running via an **isolated, local-only optical and wire bus (LAN)** executing deterministically verified (DDC, Art. 7.3) firmware locked on physically write-protected, read-only solid-state storage (ROM).
   - Local systems retain full automated stoichiometric optimization without receiving or parsing external network packets. Manual analog counterweight levers and gravity gates remain as the tertiary, emergency fail-safe against total hardware physical destruction.
3. **Passive Geometric Shielding & Inverted Thermodynamic Cost (Art. 6.3 & Art. 3.1):**
   - High-risk nodes deploy **Passive Architectural Geometric Hardening**: tensioned UHMWPE / high-tensile steel mesh canopies and modular chain curtains over greenhouses and civic plazas that passively deflect and ensnare low-cost loitering munitions with zero energetic expenditure and zero ammunition consumption.
   - High-Power Microwave emitters are powered directly from stationary DC microgrid buses and LFP battery buffers (Art. 6.2). Emitting a millisecond disabling EMP pulse consumes $\approx 0.02\text{ kWh}$, whereas the attacking drone embodies $> 15\text{ kWh}$ of manufactured mass. The thermodynamic attrition curve is thus **radically inverted in favor of the defending commons**.
4. **The Ontological Boundary Invariant (Art. 8.1.4):**
   - Machine destruction is explicitly defined as non-somatic sanitization (*Purgatio Mechanica*). Synthetic weapon systems hold zero civil status, legal personhood, or restorative standing. Defending assemblies hold the absolute right to obliterate synthetic aggressors without moral or procedural delay.

### **4. Open Questions for Future Ratification**
1. *What standardized RF frequency ranges must be permanently reserved across all bioregional microgrids for emergency civilian swarm-jamming beacons?*
2. *Should physical UHMWPE passive canopy nets be mandatory standard equipment in the open-hardware building blueprints for all Tier 1 civic food greenhouses?*  
*(Pending community sortition review on GitHub Discussions).*

## **SIM-QA-15: Internal Coherence Audit — Contradictions Between Canonical Articles**

* **Date Recorded:** 2026-10-30  
* **Epistemic Status:** `[STATUS: CANONICAL_MERGED]` (Merged 2026-10-30)  
* **Originating Context:** Cross-model constitutional review (two independent AI reviews compared). The defects listed below were found by quoting the text, not by simulation.  
* **Constitutional Articles:** Arts. 2.6.2, 4.1.3, 4.2.1, 4.5, 4.6 (new), 5.2, 7.1.2, 8.1.3, 8.1.4, 10.5.3, 11.1.1

### **1. The Friction (Textual Contradictions)**
1. **Weapons (A):** Art. 8.1.3 bans combat robotics "to humans and machines alike", but Art. 8.1.4 authorizes autonomous interception platforms and a "Standoff Counter-Strike". Art. 7.1.2 also voids the delegation of "coercive physical restraint" to autonomous agents.
2. **Threshold drift (B):** Art. 10.5.3 introduces a 66% threshold outside the canonical 75 / 60 / 50%+1 ladder.
3. **Small-panel arithmetic (C):** in a 3-person panel, 75% means unanimity, so Art. 2.6.2 ("unanimous consent of the Local Mediation Panel") hands an unappealable veto to a single citizen. No rounding rule existed for any assembly size.
4. **Survival vs. sanction (D):** Art. 5.2.2 conditioned sortition rights and dwelling tenure on labor reciprocity. Art. 1.3.3 forbids leveraging survival for "labor extraction", and Art. 4.3.2 calls civic service an "inviolable" prerogative.
5. **No interpreter (E):** many clauses declare acts "void *ab initio*", but no body was empowered to make that declaration.
6. **Association (F):** Art. 4.1.3 voids political "factions", which collides with the freedom of voluntary association in Art. 1.4.1.

### **2. Adversarial Stress-Test (RED)**
* **RED-3 (roster capture):** a faction controlling shift rosters assigns dissidents impossible duties. They "refuse" their shifts, lose sortition eligibility, and are disenfranchised legally. Clause D is a working disenfranchisement machine.
* **RED-1 (single-veto lock):** one captured panelist blocks critical infrastructure indefinitely under Art. 2.6.2.
* **RED-4 (narrative attack):** critics quote 8.1.3 against 8.1.4 to show that the "non-violent" constitution authorizes autonomous strikes. The contradiction is publicly verifiable.

### **3. Purple Patch (Merged)**
* **A:**
  * Art. 7.1.2 voids delegating *coercive force against persons* to machines; intercepting uncrewed machines is not coercion of persons.
  * Art. 8.1.3 bans weapons *intended to kill or injure persons*, with the single exception of Art. 8.1.4.
  * Art. 8.1.4: autonomous engagement only against inbound projectiles and uncrewed machines, never persons or crewed vehicles. Standoff action is narrowed to the *non-lethal disablement of uncrewed launch and control infrastructure*.
* **B:** Art. 10.5.3 now requires 75% of the Global Commons Assembly, concurred by a majority of Continental Chambers, each by 60%.
* **C:**
  * New Art. 4.5.4 (Threshold Arithmetic): the smallest whole number of votes reaching the share of *seated* members.
  * Art. 4.2.1 spells out 2-of-3 and 3-of-3.
  * Art. 2.6.2: the panel *certifies* restitution, and a refusal is appealable to a 15-citizen blind panel.
* **D:** `[RED_WINS — PROVISION WITHDRAWN]` Suspension of sortition rights is deleted. Political rights are never conditioned on labor (Art. 5.2.3). Relocation is replaced by reassignment of floor area *above* the Tier 1 shelter baseline only.
* **E:** new Art. 4.6, the Constitutional Review Jury (*Custodes Constitutionis*):
  * single-case jury of 101 citizens from non-party watersheds;
  * 60% to declare an act void;
  * *in dubio pro persona* for Chapter I;
  * one appeal to a fresh jury of 301;
  * the jury may never draft or amend law.
* **F:** Art. 4.1.3 protects association and advocacy, but voids any financing, nomination, instruction, or reward of sortition delegates.

### **4. Open Questions**
1. Should Review Jury rulings carry binding precedent or only persuasive weight?
2. Is 60% the right bar for nullity, given that nullity protects minorities (arguably a lower bar should suffice)?

## **SIM-QA-16: Demographic Inversion ("Silver Tsunami") Before Full Automation**

* **Date Recorded:** 2026-10-30  
* **Epistemic Status:** `[STATUS: SIMULATION_HYPOTHESIS]` (Registered only; no stress-test run yet)  
* **Originating Context:** Cross-model constitutional review  
* **Constitutional Articles:** Arts. 5.1, 5.2, 5.6, 10.3, 10.4

### **1. The Friction**
* Art. 5.1 distributes non-automated maintenance across able-bodied adults. Art. 5.6 exempts active caregivers *ipso jure*. Art. 5.1.4 promises that physical shifts contract toward zero as automation spreads, but sets no timeline.
* In an aging node (e.g. more than 60% of residents beyond heavy-shift capacity, plus their caregivers), the residual able-bodied cohort may face shift loads that collapse into burnout or emigration. This happens before the automation curve can compensate.

### **2. Preliminary Red Vectors (to be tested)**
* **RED-2 (exemption cascade):** caregiving exemptions grow with the elderly population. The obligation then concentrates on a shrinking cohort and creates a de facto laboring caste, which Art. 5.1.1 prohibits.
* **RED-1 (exit spiral):** young adults migrate to younger Green-status basins (Art. 10.4), which accelerates the inversion.

### **3. Hypotheses to Test**
* An inter-basin labor-and-automation reciprocity compact under Art. 10.3.
* A ceiling on the per-capita weekly shift load that triggers mandatory automation priority in Tier 2-A allocation.
* Recognition of partial-capacity contributions by older residents (e.g. supervision, teaching, ground-truthing squads).

**Next action:** quantify with the SIMONE agent-based layer (age-structured cohorts × shift demand × automation adoption curve).

## **SIM-QA-17: Organic Law Separation — Restoring Technological Neutrality**

* **Date Recorded:** 2026-10-30  
* **Epistemic Status:** `[STATUS: CANONICAL_MERGED]` (Merged 2026-10-30; Annex at [`ORGANIC_LAW_ANNEX.md`](../ORGANIC_LAW_ANNEX.md))  
* **Originating Context:** Drafting-invariant audit against [`constitution-engine` SKILL](../.agents/skills/constitution-engine/SKILL.md) invariants 5–7 (De-Sci-Fi Filter, Separation of Concerns, Technological Neutrality)  
* **Draft Annex:** [`ORGANIC_LAW_ANNEX.md`](../ORGANIC_LAW_ANNEX.md)

### **1. The Friction**
The canonical text had absorbed product-level engineering detail, much of it during SIM-QA-07/08/09:
* lithium-iron-phosphate chemistry;
* proprietary-brand radio protocols;
* "DNA/RNA printers" with cryptographic firmware;
* a 15-minute slot forfeit;
* a 500 ms purge interval;
* microwave weapon types;
* L1 trim tabs with 14-day and 36-month timers;
* 15° laser blinders;
* atmospheric percentages.

Detail placed in Chapter I is shielded by Art. 1.7 from *any* future amendment. Engineering parameters would therefore be frozen permanently.

### **2. Adversarial Stress-Test (RED)**
* **RED-5 (obsolescence lock):** a better storage chemistry or a safer biosecurity method becomes legally second-class, because replacing the named technology requires a 75% constitutional amendment, or is impossible in Chapter I.
* **RED-4 (credibility attack):** specific, unsourced engineering claims (e.g. "harmless" microwave exposure) give critics an easy factual target that discredits the whole charter.

### **3. Purple Patch (Merged)**
* The Constitution keeps only functional requirements, at Arts. 1.4.3, 3.3.3, 3.5.2, 6.2.2, 6.3.2, 6.4.2, 8.1.4, 8.2.2, 10.5.3, 11.1.1, 11.3.1, 11.4.3 and 11.6.2.
* The parameters move to the Organic Law Annex (OL-1 to OL-8), which is amendable by 60%, subject to a ratchet rule (it may never weaken a guarantee) and reviewable under Art. 4.6. The Annex is anchored by new Art. 10.6.4.
* Unverified quantitative claims are carried into the Annex labelled `PROVISIONAL — UNVERIFIED`.

## **SIM-QA-10: Hawkins-Simon Viability Under Resource Embargo**

* **Date Recorded:** 2026-10-28 (Round 2 docket)  
* **Stress-Tested:** 2026-10-30  
* **Epistemic Status:** `[STATUS: CANONICAL_MERGED]` (Merged 2026-10-30) — Verdict **`RED_WINS`**  
* **Constitutional Articles:** Arts. 3.1.5, 3.1.6 (new), 3.5.2  
* **Reproducible Evidence:** [`simone/benchmarks/sim_qa_10_embargo.jl`](../simone/benchmarks/sim_qa_10_embargo.jl) → [`results`](../simone/benchmarks/results/sim_qa_10_embargo_results.md)

### **1. Findings (computed, deterministic)**
1. **Hawkins-Simon passes, but is uninformative.**
   * 12-sector matrix: ρ(A) = 0.1523, det(I−A) = 0.9541, all leading minors > 0, (I−A)⁻¹ ≥ 0.
   * The docket's proof requirement (x ≥ 0 under a 50% input cut) holds for *any* non-negative A with ρ < 1. **It cannot fail, so it proves nothing about embargo resilience.**
2. **An embargo cannot be represented in the data.** The matrix has no import row, no primary-resource row and no capacity vector. Modeled as capacity loss, the maximum embargo depth at which Tier 1 is still fully met swings with assumed spare capacity and import dependence:
   * **e\* = 0** with no spare capacity;
   * **e\* = 0.667** with 25% spare capacity (central import scenario);
   * **e\* = 1.0** with Tier-1 sectors fully autarkic.

   In short, the result depends on parameters that do not exist in the repository.
3. **Art. 3.5 reserves as written do not close the gap.** Stocks of grain and staples bridge none of the infeasible cases. The binding gaps are district heat, water and fresh produce, which cannot be stored at that scale.
4. **Data and model defects:**
   * no Tier 3 sector and no healthcare sector;
   * shelter tagged Tier 2, contrary to Art. 3.3;
   * the coded `lexicographic_allocate` ignores intermediate demand and under-counts Tier-1 needs by 1.3–2.8×;
   * the 12-sector coefficients have no documented source.

### **2. Purple Patch (Merged)**
* **New Art. 3.1.6, Tier 1 Autarkic Closure:** an annual public audit must show that every sector directly or indirectly supplying Tier 1 needs (including shelter and healthcare) can meet full Tier 1 demand from local primary resources and installed capacity alone, with external inputs set to zero, at a published reserve margin.
* **Art. 3.5.2 (rewritten):** reserves are composed according to the audit's gaps: storable stocks, plus spare parts and consumables, plus *redundant productive capacity* for goods that cannot be stored.

### **3. Engineering Follow-Ups (SIMONE)**
1. Add import and primary-resource rows and a sector capacity vector to the matrix.
2. Add Tier 3 and healthcare sectors, and retag shelter as Tier 1.
3. Make L_min operate on (I−A)⁻¹, not on final demand alone.
4. Document a source for every coefficient in `simone/data/calibration_sources.md`.
